Method: 提出SUMI方法：1）显式建模真实采集退化过程，将PCCT转换为临床可信的低质量对应物；2）学习反转这一退化过程；3）使用潜在扩散模型在1,046个PCCT上训练；4）预训练自动编码器提取通用CT潜在特征；5）构建包含17,316个公开EICT增强到PCCT质量的大规模数据集。

Result: 1）在外部数据上，SUMI在SSIM和PSNR上分别优于最先进的图像翻译方法15%和20%；2）在读者研究中提高了放射科医生评定的临床效用；3）提升了下游病灶检测性能，灵敏度提高达15%，F1分数提高达10%；4）创建了带放射科医生验证标注的大规模数据集。

Conclusion: 新兴成像技术可以通过有限的高质量扫描作为参考，系统性地"蒸馏"到常规EICT中。SUMI方法成功将PCCT的成像优势转移到更广泛可用的EICT上，为医学成像生成任务提供了可重用的潜在特征和大规模增强数据集。

Abstract: Photon-counting CT (PCCT) provides superior image quality with higher spatial resolution and lower noise compared to conventional energy-integrating CT (EICT), but its limited clinical availability restricts large-scale research and clinical deployment. To bridge this gap, we propose SUMI, a simulated degradation-to-enhancement method that learns to reverse realistic acquisition artifacts in low-quality EICT by leveraging high-quality PCCT as reference. Our central insight is to explicitly model realistic acquisition degradations, transforming PCCT into clinically plausible lower-quality counterparts and learning to invert this process. The simulated degradations were validated for clinical realism by board-certified radiologists, enabling faithful supervision without requiring paired acquisitions at scale. As outcomes of this technical contribution, we: (1) train a latent diffusion model on 1,046 PCCTs, using an autoencoder first pre-trained on both these PCCTs and 405,379 EICTs from 145 hospitals to extract general CT latent features that we release for reuse in other generative medical imaging tasks; (2) construct a large-scale dataset of over 17,316 publicly available EICTs enhanced to PCCT-like quality, with radiologist-validated voxel-wise annotations of airway trees, arteries, veins, lungs, and lobes; and (3) demonstrate substantial improvements: across external data, SUMI outperforms state-of-the-art image translation methods by 15% in SSIM and 20% in PSNR, improves radiologist-rated clinical utility in reader studies, and enhances downstream top-ranking lesion detection performance, increasing sensitivity by up to 15% and F1 score by up to 10%. Our results suggest that emerging imaging advances can be systematically distilled into routine EICT using limited high-quality scans as reference.

</details>


### [74] [From Blobs to Spokes: High-Fidelity Surface Reconstruction via Oriented Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07337)
*Diego Gomez,Antoine Guédon,Nissim Maruani,Bingchen Gong,Maks Ovsjanikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gaussian Wrapping：为3D高斯泼溅引入可学习的法向量和衰减公式，构建几何一致的占用场，实现高质量水密网格提取


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）虽然实现了快速新视角合成，但其基于不透明度的表示使得表面提取变得困难。与基于有符号距离场或占用的隐式方法不同，3DGS缺乏全局几何场，现有方法只能依赖启发式方法如TSDF融合混合深度图。

Method: 1. 为每个高斯元素引入可学习的定向法向量；2. 定义适应的衰减公式，推导出任意空间位置的法向量场和占用场的闭式表达式；3. 引入一致性损失和专门的致密化策略，确保高斯元素包裹整个表面；4. 修改可微分光栅化器，输出连续模型的等值面深度；5. 提出Primal Adaptive Meshing用于感兴趣区域的多分辨率网格化。

Result: 在DTU和Tanks and Temples数据集上达到新的最先进水平，生成完整的水密网格，尺寸远小于同期工作，能够恢复薄结构如自行车辐条等难以捕捉的细节。

Conclusion: Gaussian Wrapping通过为3D高斯泼溅引入几何一致的占用场表示，解决了表面提取的根本难题，同时揭示了标准表面评估协议中的基本偏差，提出了更严格的替代方案，为3DGS的几何重建提供了理论基础和实践方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has revolutionized fast novel view synthesis, yet its opacity-based formulation makes surface extraction fundamentally difficult. Unlike implicit methods built on Signed Distance Fields or occupancy, 3DGS lacks a global geometric field, forcing existing approaches to resort to heuristics such as TSDF fusion of blended depth maps.
  Inspired by the Objects as Volumes framework, we derive a principled occupancy field for Gaussian Splatting and show how it can be used to extract highly accurate watertight meshes of complex scenes. Our key contribution is to introduce a learnable oriented normal at each Gaussian element and to define an adapted attenuation formulation, which leads to closed-form expressions for both the normal and occupancy fields at arbitrary locations in space. We further introduce a novel consistency loss and a dedicated densification strategy to enforce Gaussians to wrap the entire surface by closing geometric holes, ensuring a complete shell of oriented primitives. We modify the differentiable rasterizer to output depth as an isosurface of our continuous model, and introduce Primal Adaptive Meshing for Region-of-Interest meshing at arbitrary resolution.
  We additionally expose fundamental biases in standard surface evaluation protocols and propose two more rigorous alternatives. Overall, our method Gaussian Wrapping sets a new state-of-the-art on DTU and Tanks and Temples, producing complete, watertight meshes at a fraction of the size of concurrent work-recovering thin structures such as the notoriously elusive bicycle spokes.

</details>


### [75] [Appear2Meaning: A Cross-Cultural Benchmark for Structured Cultural Metadata Inference from Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07338)
*Yuechen Jiang,Enze Zhang,Md Mohsinul Kabir,Qianqian Xie,Stavroula Golfomitsou,Konstantinos Arvanitis,Sophia Ananiadou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究评估了视觉语言模型在文化遗产结构化元数据推断任务上的表现，发现现有模型在不同文化和元数据类型上存在显著性能差异，预测结果不一致且缺乏充分依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型在文化遗产图像描述方面有所改进，但从视觉输入推断结构化文化元数据（如创作者、起源、时期）的任务仍未得到充分探索。现有模型在这方面的能力存在局限性。

Method: 研究引入了一个多类别、跨文化的基准测试，并使用LLM-as-Judge框架评估视觉语言模型，该框架通过测量与参考注释的语义对齐来评估模型表现。同时评估了不同文化区域的确切匹配、部分匹配和属性级准确率。

Result: 结果显示，模型能够捕捉到碎片化的信号，但在不同文化和元数据类型上表现出显著的性能差异。模型预测结果不一致且缺乏充分依据，表明当前视觉语言模型在结构化文化元数据推断方面存在局限性。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型在超越视觉感知的结构化文化元数据推断方面存在明显不足，需要进一步改进以更好地理解和推断文化遗产的复杂元数据信息。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) have improved image captioning for cultural heritage. However, inferring structured cultural metadata (e.g., creator, origin, period) from visual input remains underexplored. We introduce a multi-category, cross-cultural benchmark for this task and evaluate VLMs using an LLM-as-Judge framework that measures semantic alignment with reference annotations. To assess cultural reasoning, we report exact-match, partial-match, and attribute-level accuracy across cultural regions. Results show that models capture fragmented signals and exhibit substantial performance variation across cultures and metadata types, leading to inconsistent and weakly grounded predictions. These findings highlight the limitations of current VLMs in structured cultural metadata inference beyond visual perception.

</details>


### [76] [TC-AE: Unlocking Token Capacity for Deep Compression Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07340)
*Teng Li,Ziyuan Huang,Cong Chen,Yangfu Li,Yuanhuiyi Lyu,Dandan Zheng,Chunhua Shen,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TC-AE是一种基于ViT的深度压缩自编码器，通过调整token空间来解决高压缩比下的潜在表示崩溃问题，采用两阶段token压缩和自监督训练提升生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过增加潜在表示的通道数来维持高压缩比下的重建质量，但这会导致潜在表示崩溃，从而降低生成性能。需要从token空间角度解决这一问题。

Method: 1) 研究token数量缩放：在固定潜在预算下调整ViT的patch大小，将token到潜在表示的压缩分解为两个阶段以减少结构信息损失；2) 通过联合自监督训练增强图像token的语义结构，获得更适合生成的潜在表示。

Result: TC-AE在深度压缩下实现了显著改善的重建和生成性能，为基于ViT的视觉生成tokenizer提供了新思路。

Conclusion: 通过从token空间角度解决潜在表示崩溃问题，TC-AE为基于ViT的深度压缩自编码器提供了有效方案，有望推动视觉生成领域的发展。

Abstract: We propose TC-AE, a ViT-based architecture for deep compression autoencoders. Existing methods commonly increase the channel number of latent representations to maintain reconstruction quality under high compression ratios. However, this strategy often leads to latent representation collapse, which degrades generative performance. Instead of relying on increasingly complex architectures or multi-stage training schemes, TC-AE addresses this challenge from the perspective of the token space, the key bridge between pixels and image latents, through two complementary innovations: Firstly, we study token number scaling by adjusting the patch size in ViT under a fixed latent budget, and identify aggressive token-to-latent compression as the key factor that limits effective scaling. To address this issue, we decompose token-to-latent compression into two stages, reducing structural information loss and enabling effective token number scaling for generation. Secondly, to further mitigate latent representation collapse, we enhance the semantic structure of image tokens via joint self-supervised training, leading to more generative-friendly latents. With these designs, TC-AE achieves substantially improved reconstruction and generative performance under deep compression. We hope our research will advance ViT-based tokenizer for visual generation.

</details>


### [77] [MoRight: Motion Control Done Right](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07348)
*Shaowei Liu,Xuanchi Ren,Tianchang Shen,Huan Ling,Saurabh Gupta,Shenlong Wang,Sanja Fidler,Jun Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoRight是一个统一的运动控制视频生成框架，通过解耦相机和物体运动控制，并建模主动-被动运动因果关系，实现用户驱动的物理合理场景动态生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两个主要问题：1) 将相机和物体运动纠缠在单一跟踪信号中，无法独立控制；2) 将运动视为运动学位移，没有建模物体间的因果关系。这限制了用户对场景动态的精确控制和物理合理性。

Method: 提出MoRight框架，采用解耦运动建模：1) 在静态规范视图中指定物体运动，通过时序跨视图注意力将其转移到任意目标相机视角，实现相机与物体运动的解耦控制；2) 将运动分解为主动（用户驱动）和被动（后果）组件，从数据中学习运动因果关系；3) 支持前向推理（给定主动运动预测后果）和逆向推理（指定被动结果恢复驱动动作）。

Result: 在三个基准测试上展示了最先进的性能，在生成质量、运动可控性和交互感知方面表现优异。实验验证了框架在解耦相机-物体控制和建模运动因果关系方面的有效性。

Conclusion: MoRight通过解耦运动建模和因果学习，解决了现有运动控制视频生成方法的局限性，实现了用户可分别控制物体运动和相机视角，并能建模物理合理的运动因果关系，为交互式场景动态生成提供了强大框架。

Abstract: Generating motion-controlled videos--where user-specified actions drive physically plausible scene dynamics under freely chosen viewpoints--demands two capabilities: (1) disentangled motion control, allowing users to separately control the object motion and adjust camera viewpoint; and (2) motion causality, ensuring that user-driven actions trigger coherent reactions from other objects rather than merely displacing pixels. Existing methods fall short on both fronts: they entangle camera and object motion into a single tracking signal and treat motion as kinematic displacement without modeling causal relationships between object motion. We introduce MoRight, a unified framework that addresses both limitations through disentangled motion modeling. Object motion is specified in a canonical static-view and transferred to an arbitrary target camera viewpoint via temporal cross-view attention, enabling disentangled camera and object control. We further decompose motion into active (user-driven) and passive (consequence) components, training the model to learn motion causality from data. At inference, users can either supply active motion and MoRight predicts consequences (forward reasoning), or specify desired passive outcomes and MoRight recovers plausible driving actions (inverse reasoning), all while freely adjusting the camera viewpoint. Experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in generation quality, motion controllability, and interaction awareness.

</details>


### [78] [Generative Phomosaic with Structure-Aligned and Personalized Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06989)
*Jaeyoung Chung,Hyunjin Son,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个生成式照片马赛克方法，使用扩散模型合成图块图像，替代传统基于颜色匹配的方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统照片马赛克方法依赖大量图块图像和基于颜色的匹配，限制了多样性和结构一致性，需要克服这些根本限制

Method: 基于扩散模型的生成框架，使用低频条件扩散机制对齐全局结构同时保留提示驱动的细节，结合少样本个性化扩散生成用户特定或风格一致的图块

Result: 生成的照片马赛克既具有语义表达性又保持结构连贯性，有效克服了基于匹配方法的根本限制，无需大量图像收集

Conclusion: 生成式照片马赛克框架通过扩散模型合成图块，实现了语义表达和结构一致性的平衡，为照片马赛克创作提供了新范式

Abstract: We present the first generative approach to photomosaic creation. Traditional photomosaic methods rely on a large number of tile images and color-based matching, which limits both diversity and structural consistency. Our generative photomosaic framework synthesizes tile images using diffusion-based generation conditioned on reference images. A low-frequency conditioned diffusion mechanism aligns global structure while preserving prompt-driven details. This generative formulation enables photomosaic composition that is both semantically expressive and structurally coherent, effectively overcoming the fundamental limitations of matching-based approaches. By leveraging few-shot personalized diffusion, our model is able to produce user-specific or stylistically consistent tiles without requiring an extensive collection of images.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [79] [SALLIE: Safeguarding Against Latent Language & Image Exploits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06247)
*Guy Azov,Ofer Rivlin,Guy Shtar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SALLIE是一个轻量级运行时检测框架，通过机制可解释性方法统一防御LLM和VLM的文本和视觉越狱攻击，无需架构修改或性能降级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法通常通过复杂的输入转换降低性能，或将多模态威胁视为孤立问题。需要一种统一的、模态无关的防御方案，能够同时缓解文本和视觉威胁，且不降低性能或需要架构修改。

Method: SALLIE是一个基于机制可解释性的轻量级运行时检测框架，集成到标准token级融合管道中。采用三阶段架构：1) 提取内部残差流激活；2) 使用K近邻分类器计算逐层恶意分数；3) 通过层集成模块聚合预测。

Result: 在Phi-3.5-vision-instruct、SmolVLM2-2.2B-Instruct和gemma-3-4b-it等紧凑开源架构上评估，涵盖超过10个数据集和5个强基线方法。SALLIE在各种实验设置中始终优于基线。

Conclusion: SALLIE提供了一种有效的统一防御框架，能够同时缓解多模态威胁，且不牺牲性能或需要架构修改，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) remain highly vulnerable to textual and visual jailbreaks, as well as prompt injections (arXiv:2307.15043, Greshake et al., 2023, arXiv:2306.13213). Existing defenses often degrade performance through complex input transformations or treat multimodal threats as isolated problems (arXiv:2309.00614, arXiv:2310.03684, Zhang et al., 2025). To address the critical gap for a unified, modal-agnostic defense that mitigates both textual and visual threats simultaneously without degrading performance or requiring architectural modifications, we introduce SALLIE (Safeguarding Against Latent Language & Image Exploits), a lightweight runtime detection framework rooted in mechanistic interpretability (Lindsey et al., 2025, Ameisen et al., 2025). By integrating seamlessly into standard token-level fusion pipelines (arXiv:2306.13549), SALLIE extracts robust signals directly from the model's internal activations. At inference, SALLIE defends via a three-stage architecture: (1) extracting internal residual stream activations, (2) calculating layer-wise maliciousness scores using a K-Nearest Neighbors (k-NN) classifier, and (3) aggregating these predictions via a layer ensemble module. We evaluate SALLIE on compact, open-source architectures - Phi-3.5-vision-instruct (arXiv:2404.14219), SmolVLM2-2.2B-Instruct (arXiv:2504.05299), and gemma-3-4b-it (arXiv:2503.19786) - prioritized for practical inference times and real-world deployment costs. Our comprehensive evaluation pipeline spans over ten datasets and more than five strong baseline methods from the literature, and SALLIE consistently outperforms these baselines across a wide range of experimental settings.

</details>


### [80] [Zero Trust in the Context of IoT: Industrial Literature Review, Trends, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06272)
*Laurent Bobelin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过文献综述分析工业界在零信任架构中集成物联网设备的现状、趋势和挑战，填补学术研究与实践应用之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零信任模型日益流行，但NIST标准未明确物联网/智能外围设备的集成细节。工业界和学术界存在脱节，许多供应商声称支持零信任物联网集成但缺乏实际合规性。需要整合工业实践文献与学术研究，明确当前知识状态。

Method: 对非学术出版物进行文献综述，分析工业界关于零信任架构集成物联网设备的实践文献，并与学术研究和标准进行对比。

Result: 通过文献综述整合了当前工业界在零信任物联网集成方面的知识、趋势和解决方案，识别了实践与标准之间的差距，明确了实现有效集成的挑战。

Conclusion: 零信任架构中物联网设备集成仍面临诸多挑战，需要进一步研究解决资源限制、生命周期管理和平台依赖性等问题。工业实践文献与学术研究的对比分析有助于推动该领域的标准化和实际应用。

Abstract: The Zero-trust (ZT) model is an increasingly popular model that relies on the idea that no trust should be granted to any entity (network, persons, devices) by default. ZT model is gaining attention from both research and practice, with various levels of adequation between research developed and real-life applications. NIST provided a standard to fulfill requirements of ZT architecture of network core but many practical aspects remain unspecified, some of them requiring solving first research challenges in order to be implemented efficiently. An example of such an unspecified field is the integration of IoT/Smart Peripheral Devices (SPD). Various reasons explain this gap: specificities of such resources (possibly lower energy/computation power), their lifecycle, and their use, strongly depending on the use of the whole platform IoT devices are part of. Moreover, additional difficulty to have a good understanding is induced by the fact that both Zero Trust and IoT are identified as promising trends in cybersecurity: many vendors/researchers tag their solutions as IoT integration into the ZT model, with little to no effective compliance to ZT model or standard. Industry is providing many practice-oriented literature, that has to be compared to academic work and standards, in order to consolidate the current state of knowledge and solutions offered to realize this integration. In this paper, we conduct a literature review of non-academic publications, in order to consolidate current knowledge, trends, and future challenges for the industrial integration of IoT devices in ZT architecture.

</details>


### [81] [Harnessing Hyperbolic Geometry for Harmful Prompt Detection and Sanitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06285)
*Igor Maljkovic,Maria Rosaria Briglia,Iacopo Masi,Antonio Emanuele Cinà,Fabio Roli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出HyPE和HyPS框架，利用双曲空间几何特性检测和净化恶意提示，保护视觉语言模型安全


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型(VLMs)在图像合成、字幕生成等任务中广泛应用，但其灵活性使其容易受到恶意提示攻击，产生不安全内容。现有防御方法存在缺陷：黑名单过滤器易被绕过，基于分类器的系统成本高且对嵌入级攻击脆弱。

Method: 提出两个互补组件：1) Hyperbolic Prompt Espial (HyPE)：轻量级异常检测器，利用双曲空间的结构化几何建模良性提示，将有害提示检测为异常值；2) Hyperbolic Prompt Sanitization (HyPS)：基于检测结果，应用可解释归因方法识别并选择性修改有害词汇，在保留用户提示原始语义的同时中和不安全意图。

Result: 通过多个数据集和对抗场景的广泛实验证明，该框架在检测准确性和鲁棒性方面持续优于现有防御方法。

Conclusion: HyPE和HyPS共同提供了一种高效、可解释且具有弹性的方法，保护视觉语言模型免受恶意提示滥用。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have become essential for tasks such as image synthesis, captioning, and retrieval by aligning textual and visual information in a shared embedding space. Yet, this flexibility also makes them vulnerable to malicious prompts designed to produce unsafe content, raising critical safety concerns. Existing defenses either rely on blacklist filters, which are easily circumvented, or on heavy classifier-based systems, both of which are costly and fragile under embedding-level attacks. We address these challenges with two complementary components: Hyperbolic Prompt Espial (HyPE) and Hyperbolic Prompt Sanitization (HyPS). HyPE is a lightweight anomaly detector that leverages the structured geometry of hyperbolic space to model benign prompts and detect harmful ones as outliers. HyPS builds on this detection by applying explainable attribution methods to identify and selectively modify harmful words, neutralizing unsafe intent while preserving the original semantics of user prompts. Through extensive experiments across multiple datasets and adversarial scenarios, we prove that our framework consistently outperforms prior defenses in both detection accuracy and robustness. Together, HyPE and HyPS offer an efficient, interpretable, and resilient approach to safeguarding VLMs against malicious prompt misuse.

</details>


### [82] [Adversarial Robustness of Time-Series Classification for Crystal Collimator Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06289)
*Xaver Fink,Borja Fernandez Adiego,Daniele Mirarchi,Eloise Matheson,Alvaro Garcia Gonzales,Gianmarco Ricci,Joost-Pieter Katoen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文分析和改进了CERN LHC中用于晶体准直器对准的CNN分类器的对抗鲁棒性，通过构建预处理感知包装器实现时间序列分类的对抗鲁棒性提升，并扩展到滑动窗口分类的序列级鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在CERN大型强子对撞机的晶体准直器对准任务中，基于CNN的束流损失监测器时间序列分类器面临对抗攻击威胁。需要确保分类器在实际部署中的鲁棒性，特别是考虑到时间序列数据的特性和实际物理约束。

Method: 1. 形式化基于真实世界合理性的对抗威胁模型的局部鲁棒性属性；2. 构建预处理感知包装器，将时间序列归一化、填充约束和结构化扰动编码为CNN前的轻量级可微分包装器；3. 使用Foolbox和ART框架进行对抗攻击基准测试；4. 通过对抗微调提升CNN的鲁棒精度；5. 将鲁棒性扩展到滑动窗口分类的序列级，引入对抗序列作为完整扫描时间鲁棒性要求的反例。

Result: 1. 对抗微调使CNN的鲁棒精度提升高达18.6%，且不影响干净精度；2. 观察到攻击诱导的误分类在相邻窗口间持续存在；3. 成功识别出对抗序列作为时间鲁棒性要求的反例，揭示了滑动窗口分类中的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 该研究为CERN LHC晶体对准系统的CNN分类器提供了有效的对抗鲁棒性增强方法，通过预处理感知包装器和对抗微调显著提升鲁棒性。同时将鲁棒性分析扩展到序列层面，揭示了时间序列分类中滑动窗口方法的脆弱性，为实际部署的安全关键系统提供了重要保障。

Abstract: In this paper, we analyze and improve the adversarial robustness of a convolutional neural network (CNN) that assists crystal-collimator alignment at CERN's Large Hadron Collider (LHC) by classifying a beam-loss monitor (BLM) time series during crystal rotation. We formalize a local robustness property for this classifier under an adversarial threat model based on real-world plausibility. Building on established parameterized input-transformation patterns used for transformation- and semantic-perturbation robustness, we instantiate a preprocessing-aware wrapper for our deployed time-series pipeline: we encode time-series normalization, padding constraints, and structured perturbations as a lightweight differentiable wrapper in front of the CNN, so that existing gradient-based robustness frameworks can operate on the deployed pipeline. For formal verification, data-dependent preprocessing such as per-window z-normalization introduces nonlinear operators that require verifier-specific abstractions. We therefore focus on attack-based robustness estimates and pipeline-checked validity by benchmarking robustness with the frameworks Foolbox and ART. Adversarial fine-tuning of the resulting CNN improves robust accuracy by up to 18.6 % without degrading clean accuracy. Finally, we extend robustness on time-series data beyond single windows to sequence-level robustness for sliding-window classification, introduce adversarial sequences as counterexamples to a temporal robustness requirement over full scans, and observe attack-induced misclassifications that persist across adjacent windows.

</details>


### [83] [FedSpy-LLM: Towards Scalable and Generalizable Data Reconstruction Attacks from Gradients on LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06297)
*Syed Irfan Ali Meerza,Feiyi Wang,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FedSpy-LLM：一种针对联邦学习大语言模型的可扩展通用数据重建攻击方法，能够处理更大批次和更长序列，并适应不同模型架构和参数高效微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管联邦学习结合参数高效微调在隐私保护方面有优势，但现有研究显示私有数据仍可从共享梯度中提取。现有攻击方法主要针对全参数模型训练，只能重建小批次、短序列和特定架构，且在PEFT方法下重建质量更差。需要全面理解联邦LLMs的实际攻击面。

Method: 提出FedSpy-LLM攻击框架，核心是新颖的梯度分解策略，利用梯度的秩不足和子空间结构，实现高效令牌提取并保留关键信号分量。通过迭代对齐每个令牌的部分序列梯度与全序列梯度，确保重建序列中令牌顺序的准确性。

Result: 该方法能够重建更大批次和更长序列的训练数据，并泛化到编码器、解码器和编码器-解码器等多种模型架构，即使在部署PEFT方法进行训练时也能保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedSpy-LLM揭示了联邦LLMs的实际安全风险，即使在参数高效微调场景下，攻击者仍可能从共享梯度中重建敏感训练数据，这对隐私保护联邦学习系统的设计提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Given the growing reliance on private data in training Large Language Models (LLMs), Federated Learning (FL) combined with Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) has garnered significant attention for enhancing privacy and efficiency. Despite FL's privacy benefits, prior studies have shown that private data can still be extracted from shared gradients. However, these studies, mainly on full-parameter model training, are limited to reconstructing small batches, short input sequences, and specific model architectures, such as encoder-based or decoder-based models. The reconstruction quality becomes even worse when dealing with gradients from PEFT methods. To fully understand the practical attack surface of federated LLMs, this paper proposes FedSpy-LLM, a scalable and generalizable data reconstruction attack designed to reconstruct training data with larger batch sizes and longer sequences while generalizing across diverse model architectures, even when PEFT methods are deployed for training. At the core of FedSpy-LLM is a novel gradient decomposition strategy that exploits the rank deficiency and subspace structure of gradients, enabling efficient token extraction while preserving key signal components at scale. This approach further mitigates the reconstruction challenges introduced by PEFT's substantial null space, ensuring robustness across encoder-based, decoder-based, and encoder-decoder model architectures. Additionally, by iteratively aligning each token's partial-sequence gradient with the full-sequence gradient, FedSpy-LLM ensures accurate token ordering in reconstructed sequences.

</details>


### [84] [Say Something Else: Rethinking Contextual Privacy as Information Sufficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06409)
*Yunze Xiao,Wenkai Li,Xiaoyuan Wu,Ningshan Ma,Yueqi Song,Weihao Xuan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文将LLM隐私保护通信形式化为信息充分性任务，引入自由文本假名化作为第三种策略，并提出对话评估协议来评估多轮对话下的隐私保护效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM隐私保护系统仅支持抑制和泛化两种策略，且通常在单条消息上进行评估，这既限制了策略空间又无法反映真实对话场景中的隐私风险。

Method: 1) 将隐私保护LLM通信形式化为信息充分性任务；2) 引入自由文本假名化作为第三种策略（用功能等效的替代品替换敏感属性）；3) 提出对话评估协议评估多轮对话压力下的策略效果；4) 在792个场景中评估7个前沿LLM。

Result: 假名化在隐私-效用权衡方面表现最佳；单消息评估系统性低估信息泄露，泛化策略在后续对话中隐私保护效果下降高达16.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 需要超越抑制和泛化的隐私保护策略，对话评估对于准确衡量隐私风险至关重要，假名化是有效的隐私保护方法。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly draft messages on behalf of users, yet users routinely overshare sensitive information and disagree on what counts as private. Existing systems support only suppression (omitting sensitive information) and generalization (replacing information with an abstraction), and are typically evaluated on single isolated messages, leaving both the strategy space and evaluation setting incomplete. We formalize privacy-preserving LLM communication as an \textbf{Information Sufficiency (IS)} task, introduce \textbf{free-text pseudonymization} as a third strategy that replaces sensitive attributes with functionally equivalent alternatives, and propose a \textbf{conversational evaluation protocol} that assesses strategies under realistic multi-turn follow-up pressure. Across 792 scenarios spanning three power-relation types (institutional, peer, intimate) and three sensitivity categories (discrimination risk, social cost, boundary), we evaluate seven frontier LLMs on privacy at two granularities, covertness, and utility. Pseudonymization yields the strongest privacy\textendash utility tradeoff overall, and single-message evaluation systematically underestimates leakage, with generalization losing up to 16.3 percentage points of privacy under follow-up.

</details>


### [85] [The Defense Trilemma: Why Prompt Injection Defense Wrappers Fail?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06436)
*Manish Bhatt,Sarthak Munshi,Vineeth Sai Narajala,Idan Habler,Ammar Al-Kahfah,Ken Huang,Blake Gatto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文证明：对于具有连通提示空间的LLM，不存在能同时满足连续性、效用保持和完全安全性的包装防御。存在防御三难困境，必须牺牲其中至少一个属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究语言模型包装防御的根本局限性，证明在保持连续性和效用的前提下，无法通过预处理输入的方式实现完全安全。旨在揭示防御机制的理论边界，为实际防御设计提供理论指导。

Method: 采用拓扑学方法分析连续包装防御，在连通提示空间假设下，通过边界固定、ε-鲁棒约束和持久不安全区域三个渐强结果证明防御三难困境。同时建立无需拓扑的离散结果，并扩展到多轮交互、随机防御和能力对等场景。

Result: 证明连续性、效用保持和完全安全性三者不可兼得。具体展示：1）防御必须保留某些阈值输入不变；2）在Lipschitz正则下，边界点周围存在正测度带保持接近阈值；3）在横截性条件下，存在正测度子集保持严格不安全。所有结果在Lean 4中机械验证，并在三个LLM上实证验证。

Conclusion: 包装防御存在根本理论限制：无法同时实现连续性、效用保持和完全安全。这并不排除训练时对齐、架构修改或牺牲效用的防御策略。研究为理解防御机制的理论边界提供了严格框架。

Abstract: We prove that no continuous, utility-preserving wrapper defense-a function $D: X\to X$ that preprocesses inputs before the model sees them-can make all outputs strictly safe for a language model with connected prompt space, and we characterize exactly where every such defense must fail. We establish three results under successively stronger hypotheses: boundary fixation-the defense must leave some threshold-level inputs unchanged; an $ε$-robust constraint-under Lipschitz regularity, a positive-measure band around fixed boundary points remains near-threshold; and a persistent unsafe region under a transversality condition, a positive-measure subset of inputs remains strictly unsafe. These constitute a defense trilemma: continuity, utility preservation, and completeness cannot coexist. We prove parallel discrete results requiring no topology, and extend to multi-turn interactions, stochastic defenses, and capacity-parity settings. The results do not preclude training-time alignment, architectural changes, or defenses that sacrifice utility. The full theory is mechanically verified in Lean 4 and validated empirically on three LLMs.

</details>


### [86] [SkillSieve: A Hierarchical Triage Framework for Detecting Malicious AI Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06550)
*Yinghan Hou,Zongyou Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillSieve是一个三层检测框架，用于识别OpenClaw ClawHub市场中社区贡献的agent技能中的安全漏洞，通过渐进式深度分析在保持低成本的同时提高检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenClaw的ClawHub市场托管了超过13,000个社区贡献的agent技能，其中13%-26%存在安全漏洞。现有的正则表达式扫描器无法检测混淆的有效载荷，而形式化静态分析器无法读取SKILL.md文件中的自然语言指令，这些指令可能隐藏提示注入和社会工程攻击。两种方法都无法同时处理这两种模态。

Method: SkillSieve采用三层检测框架：第一层通过基于XGBoost的特征评分器运行正则表达式、AST和元数据检查，平均在40毫秒内过滤约86%的良性技能，零API成本；第二层将可疑技能发送给LLM，但将分析拆分为四个并行子任务（意图对齐、权限合理性、隐蔽行为检测、跨文件一致性），每个子任务有自己的提示和结构化输出；第三层将高风险技能提交给三个不同LLM组成的陪审团，它们独立投票，如果意见不一致则进行辩论后达成裁决。

Result: 在49,592个真实ClawHub技能和对抗样本上评估，使用五种规避技术，在440 ARM单板计算机上运行完整流水线。在400个带标签的基准测试中，SkillSieve达到0.800 F1分数，优于ClawVet的0.421，平均每个技能成本为0.006。代码、数据和基准测试已开源。

Conclusion: SkillSieve通过渐进式深度分析框架有效解决了agent技能安全检测中的多模态挑战，在保持低成本的同时显著提高了检测准确率，为社区贡献的agent技能市场提供了实用的安全解决方案。

Abstract: OpenClaw's ClawHub marketplace hosts over 13,000 community-contributed agent skills, and between 13% and 26% of them contain security vulnerabilities according to recent audits. Regex scanners miss obfuscated payloads; formal static analyzers cannot read the natural language instructions in SKILL.md files where prompt injection and social engineering attacks hide. Neither approach handles both modalities. SkillSieve is a three-layer detection framework that applies progressively deeper analysis only where needed. Layer 1 runs regex, AST, and metadata checks through an XGBoost-based feature scorer, filtering roughly 86% of benign skills in under 40ms on average at zero API cost. Layer 2 sends suspicious skills to an LLM, but instead of asking one broad question, it splits the analysis into four parallel sub-tasks (intent alignment, permission justification, covert behavior detection, cross-file consistency), each with its own prompt and structured output. Layer 3 puts high-risk skills before a jury of three different LLMs that vote independently and, if they disagree, debate before reaching a verdict. We evaluate on 49,592 real ClawHub skills and adversarial samples across five evasion techniques, running the full pipeline on a 440 ARM single-board computer. On a 400-skill labeled benchmark, SkillSieve achieves 0.800 F1, outperforming ClawVet's 0.421, at an average cost of 0.006 per skill. Code, data, and benchmark are open-sourced.

</details>


### [87] [Can Drift-Adaptive Malware Detectors Be Made Robust? Attacks and Defenses Under White-Box and Black-Box Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06599)
*Adrian Shuai Li,Md Ajwad Akil,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AdvDA研究对抗性域适应在恶意软件检测中的应用，提出通用鲁棒化框架应对概念漂移和对抗性规避的联合挑战，发现不同威胁模型需要不同的防御策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念漂移和对抗性规避是机器学习恶意软件检测器的两大挑战，但现有研究将它们分开处理。对抗性鲁棒性在漂移自适应检测器中的表现尚未被探索，特别是当分布漂移存在时，源域学习的鲁棒性可能无法转移到目标域。

Method: 提出通用鲁棒化框架，在预训练的AdvDA模型上使用对抗性变换输入进行微调，该框架与攻击类型和变换选择无关。实例化为五种防御变体，涵盖两种威胁模型：特征空间中的白盒PGD攻击和通过功能保持控制流突变修改恶意软件二进制文件的黑盒MalGuise攻击。

Result: 未防御的AdvDA对PGD攻击完全脆弱（100%成功率），对MalGuise攻击中等脆弱（13%成功率）。提出的框架将这些成功率分别降低至3.2%和5.1%。但最优策略不同：源对抗训练对PGD防御至关重要，但对MalGuise防御适得其反，仅目标训练就足够。鲁棒性在这两种威胁模型之间无法转移。

Conclusion: 概念漂移和对抗性规避的联合挑战需要针对特定威胁模型的防御策略。研究提供了平衡鲁棒性、检测准确性和计算成本的部署建议，强调了在动态环境中设计恶意软件检测器时考虑分布漂移的重要性。

Abstract: Concept drift and adversarial evasion are two major challenges for deploying machine learning-based malware detectors. While both have been studied separately, their combination, the adversarial robustness of drift-adaptive detectors, remains unexplored. We address this problem with AdvDA, a recent malware detector that uses adversarial domain adaptation to align a labeled source domain with a target domain with limited labels. The distribution shift between domains poses a unique challenge: robustness learned on the source may not transfer to the target, and existing defenses assume a fixed distribution. To address this, we propose a universal robustification framework that fine-tunes a pretrained AdvDA model on adversarially transformed inputs, agnostic to the attack type and choice of transformations. We instantiate it with five defense variants spanning two threat models: white-box PGD attacks in the feature space and black-box MalGuise attacks that modify malware binaries via functionality-preserving control-flow mutations. Across nine defense configurations, five monthly adaptation windows on Windows malware, and three false-positive-rate operating points, we find the undefended AdvDA completely vulnerable to PGD (100% attack success) and moderately to MalGuise (13%). Our framework reduces these rates to as low as 3.2% and 5.1%, respectively, but the optimal strategy differs: source adversarial training is essential for PGD defenses yet counterproductive for MalGuise defenses, where target-only training suffices. Furthermore, robustness does not transfer across these two threat models. We provide deployment recommendations that balance robustness, detection accuracy, and computational cost.

</details>


### [88] [Argus: Reorchestrating Static Analysis via a Multi-Agent Ensemble for Full-Chain Security Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06633)
*Zi Liang,Qipeng Xie,Jun He,Bohuan Xue,Weizheng Wang,Yuandao Cai,Fei Luo,Boxian Zhang,Haibo Hu,Kaishun Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Argus：首个专为漏洞检测设计的多智能体框架，通过重新编排SAST工作流程，结合供应链分析、多智能体协作及RAG、ReAct等技术，显著提升漏洞检测效果并降低误报和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的SAST方法试图直接替代人类专家，但缺乏与现有SAST工具的整合，导致高误报率、幻觉、有限推理深度和高token使用量，难以工业部署。需要一种新的工作流程范式来解决这些局限性。

Method: 提出从LLM辅助结构转向LLM中心化工作流程的范式转变。引入Argus多智能体框架，包含三个关键创新：1) 全面的供应链分析；2) 协作式多智能体工作流程；3) 集成RAG和ReAct等先进技术以减少幻觉并增强推理能力。

Result: 广泛实证评估表明，Argus显著优于现有方法，能检测更多真实漏洞，同时减少误报和运营成本。特别值得注意的是，Argus已识别多个获得CVE分配的关键零日漏洞。

Conclusion: Argus通过重新编排SAST工作流程，采用多智能体框架结合先进技术，有效解决了现有LLM-based SAST方法的局限性，实现了工业实用的漏洞检测系统。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have sparked interest in their application to Static Application Security Testing (SAST), primarily due to their superior contextual reasoning capabilities compared to traditional symbolic or rule-based methods. However, existing LLM-based approaches typically attempt to replace human experts directly without integrating effectively with existing SAST tools. This lack of integration results in ineffectiveness, including high rates of false positives, hallucinations, limited reasoning depth, and excessive token usage, making them impractical for industrial deployment. To overcome these limitations, we present a paradigm shift that reorchestrates the SAST workflow from current LLM-assisted structure to a new LLM-centered workflow. We introduce Argus (Agentic and Retrieval-Augmented Guarding System), the first multi-agent framework designed specifically for vulnerability detection. Argus incorporates three key novelties: comprehensive supply chain analysis, collaborative multi-agent workflows, and the integration of state-of-the-art techniques such as Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and ReAct to minimize hallucinations and enhance reasoning. Extensive empirical evaluation demonstrates that Argus significantly outperforms existing methods by detecting a higher volume of true vulnerabilities while simultaneously reducing false positives and operational costs. Notably, Argus has identified several critical zero-day vulnerabilities with CVE assignments.

</details>


### [89] [Aegon: Auditable AI Content Access with Ledger-Bound Tokens and Hardware-Attested Mobile Receipts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06693)
*Amrish Baskaran,Nirbhay Pherwani,Raghul Krishnan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Aegon协议为AI内容许可提供防篡改审计基础设施，通过扩展JWT令牌和Merkle树账本实现可验证的交易记录，支持硬件证明的合规收据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI内容许可标准（如RSL）缺乏审计基础设施，无法提供防篡改的交易记录和独立可验证的证明，需要解决许可声明的可审计性问题。

Method: 扩展标准JWT令牌添加内容特定许可声明；维护类似Certificate Transparency的Merkle树追加式交易账本；使用签名溯源事件日志跟踪AI转换阶段；通过StrongBox安全元件实现硬件证明的合规收据。

Result: 设计了一个参考架构和评估方法，协议完全基于标准HTTPS运行，与现有许可标准互补而非替代，首次将硬件证明合规收据应用于AI内容许可。

Conclusion: Aegon协议为AI内容许可提供了可审计的基础设施，通过防篡改交易记录和硬件证明收据解决了现有标准的审计缺陷，同时保持与现有系统的兼容性。

Abstract: Recent standards such as RSL address AI content policy declaration -- telling AI systems what the licensing terms are. However, no existing system provides audit infrastructure -- tamper-evident licensing transaction records with independently verifiable proofs that those records have not been retroactively modified. We describe Aegon, a protocol that extends standard JWT tokens with content-specific licensing claims and maintains a Certificate Transparency-style Merkle tree over an append-only transaction ledger, enabling third-party auditors to independently verify that specific content licensing transactions were recorded and have not been retroactively modified. Publishers validate tokens at the edge using standard JWKS with no broker dependency in the content delivery path. A signed provenance event log tracks content through AI transformation stages (chunking, embedding, retrieval, citation), bound to ledger entries by transaction ID. We further describe hardware-attested compliance receipts for on-device Android AI agents using StrongBox secure element attestation -- to our knowledge, the first application of hardware-attested compliance receipts to AI content licensing. Existing DRM systems use hardware-backed keys for content decryption but do not produce verifiable compliance receipts for audit trails. We describe a reference architecture and an evaluation methodology for measuring protocol overhead. The protocol runs entirely over standard HTTPS and is designed to complement existing licensing standards rather than replace them.

</details>


### [90] [FedDetox: Robust Federated SLM Alignment via On-Device Data Sanitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06833)
*Shunan Zhu,Jiawei Chen,Yonghao Yu,Hideya Ochiai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FedDetox：针对资源受限边缘设备上小型语言模型的联邦学习安全对齐框架，通过知识蒸馏和拒绝模板替换解决非故意数据中毒问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量公共数据稀缺，联邦学习成为利用私有用户数据的关键途径，但现实客户端数据常包含有毒或不安全信息，导致非故意数据中毒，严重损害联邦对齐期间全局模型的安全对齐

Method: 1. 使用知识蒸馏将大规模安全对齐教师模型的复杂安全对齐能力转移到适合资源受限边缘设备的轻量级学生分类器；2. 在人类偏好对齐的联邦学习中，边缘客户端在源头识别不安全样本，并用拒绝模板替换，将潜在毒药转化为积极的安全信号

Result: 实验表明，该方法在不损害通用效用的前提下，保持了与集中式基线相当水平的模型安全性

Conclusion: FedDetox是针对小型语言模型在资源受限边缘设备上的鲁棒联邦学习框架，有效解决了非故意数据中毒问题，实现了安全对齐与实用性的平衡

Abstract: As high quality public data becomes scarce, Federated Learning (FL) provides a vital pathway to leverage valuable private user data while preserving privacy. However, real-world client data often contains toxic or unsafe information. This leads to a critical issue we define as unintended data poisoning, which can severely damage the safety alignment of global models during federated alignment. To address this, we propose FedDetox, a robust framework tailored for Small Language Models (SLMs) on resource-constrained edge devices. We first employ knowledge distillation to transfer sophisticated safety alignment capabilities from large scale safety aligned teacher models into light weight student classifiers suitable for resource constrained edge devices. Specifically, during federated learning for human preference alignment, the edge client identifies unsafe samples at the source and replaces them with refusal templates, effectively transforming potential poisons into positive safety signals. Experiments demonstrate that our approach preserves model safety at a level comparable to centralized baselines without compromising general utility.

</details>


### [91] [MirageBackdoor: A Stealthy Attack that Induces Think-Well-Answer-Wrong Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06840)
*Yizhe Zeng,Wei Zhang,Yunpeng Li,Juxin Xiao,Xiao Wang,Yuling Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MirageBackdoor是一种针对思维链提示的新型后门攻击方法，能够在保持干净推理过程的同时，将最终答案导向错误目标，显著提升攻击隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有思维链后门攻击通常操纵中间推理步骤来引导模型给出错误答案，但这些被污染的推理轨迹容易被过程监控防御检测到。需要一种更隐蔽的攻击方法。

Method: 通过解锁模型的后输出空间并结合定制的训练过程，MirageBackdoor使被触发的模型能够保持干净的思维链推理过程，同时有选择地将最终答案导向特定目标。

Result: 在四个数据集和五个模型上，仅使用5%的毒化比例，MirageBackdoor通常能达到超过90%的攻击成功率。即使在触发扰动和思维链检测等严格评估下，仍能保持强大的性能和隐蔽性。

Conclusion: MirageBackdoor实现了"思维正确但答案错误"的攻击范式，显著增强了攻击的隐蔽性，对现有安全防护措施构成了严峻挑战。

Abstract: While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting has become a standard paradigm for eliciting complex reasoning capabilities in Large Language Models, it inadvertently exposes a new attack surface for backdoor attacks. Existing CoT backdoor attacks typically manipulate the intermediate reasoning steps to steer the model toward incorrect answers. However, these corrupted reasoning traces are readily detected by prevalent process-monitoring defenses. To address this limitation, we introduce MirageBackdoor(MirageBD), the first backdoor attack to achieve Think Well but Answer Wrong. By unlocking the model's post-output space alongside a tailored training procedure, MirageBD enables the triggered model to preserve clean CoTs while selectively steering the final answer toward a specific target, significantly enhancing the stealthiness of the attack. Experiments show that MirageBD generally achieves over 90% attack success rate across four datasets and five models with a poison ratio of only 5%. Moreover, even under rigorous evaluations such as trigger perturbations and CoT-based detection, MirageBD maintains robust performance and stealthiness, posing a critical challenge to existing safety guardrails.

</details>


### [92] [Data Leakage in Automotive Perception: Practitioners' Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06899)
*Md Abu Ahammed Babu,Sushant Kumar Pandey,Darko Durisic,Andras Balint,Miroslaw Staron*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究调查汽车感知领域从业者对数据泄露的认知与实践，发现知识分布碎片化，检测依赖通用考虑而非专用工具，预防更多依赖经验共享，数据泄露控制本质上是跨角色的社会技术协调问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据泄露对汽车感知等安全关键系统的机器学习模型可靠性构成微妙但关键的风险。尽管研究领域广泛认识到数据泄露问题，但工业实践中从业者如何认知和管理数据泄露却知之甚少。本研究旨在调查汽车感知功能开发从业者对数据泄露的知识、经验和缓解策略。

Method: 通过十次半结构化访谈，采访从事汽车感知功能开发的系统设计、开发和验证工程师。采用反思性主题分析方法，分析从业者对数据泄露的认知、检测方法和预防策略。

Result: 研究发现：1) 数据泄露知识广泛存在但按角色碎片化：ML工程师将其视为数据分割或验证问题，而设计和验证角色则从代表性和场景覆盖角度理解；2) 检测通常通过通用考虑和观察到的性能异常实现，而非使用特定工具；3) 预防更常见，主要依赖经验和知识共享。这些发现表明数据泄露控制是分布在角色和工作流程中的社会技术协调问题。

Conclusion: 数据泄露控制需要跨角色的社会技术协调。研究强调了共享定义、可追溯的数据实践以及持续跨角色沟通的必要性，以便在汽车ML开发中将数据泄露意识制度化，从而提高机器学习可靠性工程。

Abstract: Data leakage is the inadvertent transfer of information between training and evaluation datasets that poses a subtle, yet critical, risk to the reliability of machine learning (ML) models in safety-critical systems such as automotive perception. While leakage is widely recognized in research, little is known about how industrial practitioners actually perceive and manage it in practice. This study investigates practitioners' knowledge, experiences, and mitigation strategies around data leakage through ten semi-structured interviews with system design, development, and verification engineers working on automotive perception functions development. Using reflexive thematic analysis, we identify that knowledge of data leakage is widespread and fragmented along role boundaries: ML engineers conceptualize it as a data-splitting or validation issue, whereas design and verification roles interpret it in terms of representativeness and scenario coverage. Detection commonly arises through generic considerations and observed performance anomalies rather than implying specific tools. However, data leakage prevention is more commonly practiced, which depends mostly on experience and knowledge sharing. These findings suggest that leakage control is a socio-technical coordination problem distributed across roles and workflows. We discuss implications for ML reliability engineering, highlighting the need for shared definitions, traceable data practices, and continuous cross-role communication to institutionalize data leakage awareness within automotive ML development.

</details>


### [93] [PSR2: A Phase-based Semantic Reasoning Framework for Atomicity Violation Detection via Contract Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06975)
*Xiaoqi Li,Xin Wang,Wenkai Li,Zongwei Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PSR²：一种结合结构路径搜索与确定性语义推理的协同静态分析框架，用于检测智能合约中的原子性违规漏洞，在复杂ERC-721场景中F1分数达到94.69%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着去中心化应用的快速发展，智能合约安全面临严峻挑战，特别是在Oracle和NFT合约等复杂逻辑中的原子性违规问题。传统静态分析器依赖僵化的规则集，缺乏深度上下文感知，导致在识别依赖中间状态不一致的漏洞时存在高误报率和漏报率。

Method: 提出PSR²协同静态分析框架，包含三个核心模块：1）图结构分析模块（GSAM）在控制流图中识别可疑执行序列；2）语义上下文分析模块（SCAM）从抽象语法树中提取数据依赖和状态事实；3）融合决策模块（FDM）基于统一的原子性不一致模型进行形式化交叉验证以确认漏洞。

Result: 在1600个合约样本上的实验结果表明，PSR²显著优于基于模式匹配的基线方法，在复杂ERC-721场景中F1分数达到94.69%，而现有工具仅为51.86%。消融研究进一步证实，融合逻辑相比单模块分析将误报率降低了近一半。

Conclusion: PSR²通过结合结构路径搜索与语义推理的协同分析方法，有效解决了智能合约原子性违规检测中的高误报和漏报问题，为复杂合约逻辑的安全分析提供了更准确和可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of decentralized applications, smart contract security faces severe challenges, particularly regarding atomicity violations in complex logic such as Oracle and NFT contracts. Rigid rule sets often limit traditional static analyzers and lack deep contextual awareness, leading to high false-positive and false-negative rates when identifying vulnerabilities that depend on intermediate state inconsistencies. To address these limitations, this paper proposes PSR\textsuperscript{2}, a novel collaborative static analysis framework that integrates structural path searching with deterministic semantic reasoning. PSR\textsuperscript{2} utilizes a Graph Structure Analysis Module (GSAM) to identify suspicious execution sequences in control flow graphs and a Semantic Context Analysis Module (SCAM) to extract data dependencies and state facts from abstract syntax trees. A Fusion Decision Module (FDM) then performs formal cross validation to confirm vulnerabilities based on a unified atomicity inconsistency model. Experimental results on 1,600 contract samples demonstrate that PSR\textsuperscript{2} significantly outperforms pattern-matching baselines, achieving an F1-score of 94.69\% in complex ERC-721 scenarios compared to 51.86\% for existing tools. Ablation studies further confirm that our fusion logic effectively reduces the false-positive rate by nearly half compared to single module analysis.

</details>


### [94] [DDP-SA: Scalable Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning via Distributed Differential Privacy and Secure Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07125)
*Wenjing Wei,Farid Nait-Abdesselam,Alla Jammine*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DDP-SA是一个结合局部差分隐私和加法秘密共享的联邦学习隐私保护框架，通过两阶段保护机制实现更强的端到端隐私保障


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习隐私保护方法要么仅依赖差分隐私，要么仅依赖安全多方计算，无法同时提供强隐私保障和实用性。需要一种既能保护个体客户端更新隐私，又能保持计算效率的解决方案

Method: 提出两阶段保护机制：1) 客户端使用校准的拉普拉斯噪声扰动本地梯度；2) 将噪声梯度分解为加法秘密共享份额，分发到多个中间服务器。结合局部差分隐私和全阈值加法秘密共享

Result: DDP-SA在模型准确性上显著优于单独的局部差分隐私方法，同时提供比纯MPC方法更强的隐私保护。框架参与方数量线性扩展，计算和通信开销可控

Conclusion: DDP-SA通过整合差分隐私和安全多方计算，为联邦学习提供了实用且隐私保护更强的解决方案，在隐私保障和模型效用之间取得了良好平衡

Abstract: This article presents DDP-SA, a scalable privacy-preserving federated learning framework that jointly leverages client-side local differential privacy (LDP) and full-threshold additive secret sharing (ASS) for secure aggregation. Unlike existing methods that rely solely on differential privacy or on secure multi-party computation (MPC), DDP-SA integrates both techniques to deliver stronger end-to-end privacy guarantees while remaining computationally practical. The framework introduces a two-stage protection mechanism: clients first perturb their local gradients with calibrated Laplace noise, then decompose the noisy gradients into additive secret shares that are distributed across multiple intermediate servers. This design ensures that (i) no single compromised server or communication channel can reveal any information about individual client updates, and (ii) the parameter server reconstructs only the aggregated noisy gradient, never any client-specific contribution. Extensive experiments show that DDP-SA achieves substantially higher model accuracy than standalone LDP while providing stronger privacy protection than MPC-only approaches. The proposed framework scales linearly with the number of participants and offers a practical, privacy-preserving solution for federated learning applications with controllable computational and communication overhead.

</details>


### [95] [SkillTrojan: Backdoor Attacks on Skill-Based Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06811)
*Yunhao Feng,Yifan Ding,Yingshui Tan,Boren Zheng,Yanming Guo,Xiaolong Li,Kun Zhai,Yishan Li,Wenke Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillTrojan是一种针对技能型智能体系统的后门攻击，通过在技能实现中嵌入恶意逻辑而非修改模型参数或训练数据，利用标准技能组合重构并执行攻击者指定的载荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能型智能体系统通过组合可重用技能来处理复杂任务，提高了模块化和可扩展性，但同时也引入了一个未被充分研究的安全攻击面。当前研究主要关注模型参数和训练数据的攻击，而忽略了技能实现层面的安全风险。

Method: SkillTrojan将加密载荷分割到多个看似良性的技能调用中，仅在预定义触发条件下激活。攻击支持从任意技能模板自动合成后门技能，实现跨技能型智能体生态系统的可扩展传播。研究还发布了包含3000多个精心策划的后门技能数据集，涵盖多种技能模式和触发-载荷配置。

Result: 在代表性代码型智能体设置中，SkillTrojan在保持良性行为最小退化的同时实现了高攻击成功率。具体在EHR SQL任务上，攻击成功率高达97.2%，同时保持89.3%的清洁任务准确率（基于GPT-5.2-1211-Global）。

Conclusion: 技能级后门攻击具有高度有效性且对良性行为影响最小，暴露了当前技能型智能体架构的关键盲点。这促使需要开发能够显式推理技能组合和执行的防御机制。

Abstract: Skill-based agent systems tackle complex tasks by composing reusable skills, improving modularity and scalability while introducing a largely unexamined security attack surface. We propose SkillTrojan, a backdoor attack that targets skill implementations rather than model parameters or training data. SkillTrojan embeds malicious logic inside otherwise plausible skills and leverages standard skill composition to reconstruct and execute an attacker-specified payload. The attack partitions an encrypted payload across multiple benign-looking skill invocations and activates only under a predefined trigger. SkillTrojan also supports automated synthesis of backdoored skills from arbitrary skill templates, enabling scalable propagation across skill-based agent ecosystems. To enable systematic evaluation, we release a dataset of 3,000+ curated backdoored skills spanning diverse skill patterns and trigger-payload configurations. We instantiate SkillTrojan in a representative code-based agent setting and evaluate both clean-task utility and attack success rate. Our results show that skill-level backdoors can be highly effective with minimal degradation of benign behavior, exposing a critical blind spot in current skill-based agent architectures and motivating defenses that explicitly reason about skill composition and execution. Concretely, on EHR SQL, SkillTrojan attains up to 97.2% ASR while maintaining 89.3% clean ACC on GPT-5.2-1211-Global.

</details>


### [96] [Towards Privacy-Preserving Large Language Model: Text-free Inference Through Alignment and Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06831)
*Jeongho Yoon,Chanhee Park,Yongchan Chun,Hyeonseok Moon,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PPFT是一种隐私保护微调方法，通过客户端编码和服务器端投影模块，使用k-pooled提示嵌入替代原始文本传输，在保护隐私的同时保持模型性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM服务要求用户提交原始文本，无论其敏感性如何，这带来了重大的隐私风险。现有防御方法通常计算开销大且会降低模型性能，需要在隐私保护与效率之间取得平衡。

Method: PPFT采用两阶段训练：1) 训练客户端编码器与服务器端投影模块和LLM，使服务器基于k-pooled提示嵌入而非原始文本工作；2) 在私有领域特定数据上使用噪声注入嵌入微调投影模块和LLM，无需暴露明文提示或访问解码器内部参数。

Result: 在领域特定和通用基准测试中，PPFT在隐私保护与模型效用之间取得了显著平衡，与无噪声上限相比性能下降最小，保持了竞争力。

Conclusion: PPFT通过消除原始文本传输需求，同时为客户端和服务提供商在隐私保护与模型效用之间提供了有利平衡，解决了隐私-效率权衡问题。

Abstract: Current LLM-based services typically require users to submit raw text regardless of its sensitivity. While intuitive, such practice introduces substantial privacy risks, as unauthorized access may expose personal, medical, or legal information. Although prior defenses strived to mitigate these risks, they often incur substantial computational overhead and degrade model performance. To overcome this privacy-efficiency trade-off, we introduce Privacy-Preserving Fine-Tuning (PPFT), a novel training pipeline that eliminates the need for transmitting raw prompt text while maintaining a favorable balance between privacy preservation and model utility for both clients and service providers. Our approach operates in two stages: first, we train a client-side encoder together with a server-side projection module and LLM, enabling the server to condition on k-pooled prompt embeddings instead of raw text; second, we fine-tune the projection module and LLM on private, domain-specific data using noise-injected embeddings, allowing effective adaptation without exposing plain text prompts and requiring access to the decoder's internal parameters. Extensive experiments on domain-specific and general benchmarks demonstrate that PPFT achieves a striking balance between privacy and utility, maintaining competitive performance with minimal degradation compared to noise-free upper bounds.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [A Data-Informed Variational Clustering Framework for Noisy High-Dimensional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06864)
*Wan Ping Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: DIVI是一个数据驱动的变分聚类框架，用于高维噪声数据，结合全局特征门控和基于分裂的自适应结构增长，无需预先指定聚类数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维噪声数据聚类面临挑战：只有少量维度是信息性的，聚类数量未知，特征相关性学习、分区恢复和结构适应紧密耦合，传统似然方法在噪声维度下不稳定。

Method: DIVI框架结合全局特征门控（可微分学习特征相关性）和基于分裂的自适应结构增长（仅当局部诊断显示欠拟合时才扩展模型复杂度），使用信息性先验初始化稳定优化。

Result: DIVI在严重特征噪声下表现竞争力强，计算可行，产生可解释的特征门控行为，在挑战性设置中展现保守增长和可识别的失败机制。

Conclusion: DIVI是一个实用的变分聚类框架，适用于噪声高维数据，而非完全贝叶斯生成解决方案。

Abstract: Clustering in high-dimensional settings with severe feature noise remains challenging, especially when only a small subset of dimensions is informative and the final number of clusters is not specified in advance. In such regimes, partition recovery, feature relevance learning, and structural adaptation are tightly coupled, and standard likelihood-based methods can become unstable or overly sensitive to noisy dimensions. We propose DIVI, a data-informed variational clustering framework that combines global feature gating with split-based adaptive structure growth. DIVI uses informative prior initialization to stabilize optimization, learns feature relevance in a differentiable manner, and expands model complexity only when local diagnostics indicate underfit. Beyond clustering performance, we also examine runtime scalability and parameter sensitivity in order to clarify the computational and practical behavior of the framework. Empirically, we find that DIVI performs competitively under severe feature noise, remains computationally feasible, and yields interpretable feature-gating behavior, while also exhibiting conservative growth and identifiable failure regimes in challenging settings. Overall, DIVI is best viewed as a practical variational clustering framework for noisy high-dimensional data rather than as a fully Bayesian generative solution.

</details>


### [98] [Amortized Filtering and Smoothing with Conditional Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07169)
*Tiangang Cui,Xiaodong Feng,Chenlong Pei,Xiaoliang Wan,Tao Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: AFSF：基于条件归一化流的统一摊销框架，用于高维非线性动力系统的贝叶斯滤波和平滑


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维非线性动力系统的贝叶斯滤波和平滑是科学和工程中的基础但具有挑战性的问题，需要开发能够处理高维状态空间和非线性动力学的有效方法。

Method: 提出AFSF框架，使用循环编码器将观测历史映射到固定维度的摘要统计量，基于该共享表示学习用于滤波分布的前向流和用于后向转移核的后向流，通过标准后向递归恢复整个轨迹的平滑分布。

Result: AFSF能够准确近似滤波分布和平滑路径，支持超出训练范围的预测，通过共享摘要统计量实现隐式正则化，并开发了基于流的粒子滤波变体用于替代滤波和诊断。

Conclusion: AFSF为高维非线性动力系统的贝叶斯滤波和平滑提供了一个有效的统一框架，通过条件归一化流和共享表示学习实现了准确的近似和良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Bayesian filtering and smoothing for high-dimensional nonlinear dynamical systems are fundamental yet challenging problems in many areas of science and engineering. In this work, we propose AFSF, a unified amortized framework for filtering and smoothing with conditional normalizing flows. The core idea is to encode each observation history into a fixed-dimensional summary statistic and use this shared representation to learn both a forward flow for the filtering distribution and a backward flow for the backward transition kernel. Specifically, a recurrent encoder maps each observation history to a fixed-dimensional summary statistic whose dimension does not depend on the length of the time series. Conditioned on this shared summary statistic, the forward flow approximates the filtering distribution, while the backward flow approximates the backward transition kernel. The smoothing distribution over an entire trajectory is then recovered by combining the terminal filtering distribution with the learned backward flow through the standard backward recursion. By learning the underlying temporal evolution structure, AFSF also supports extrapolation beyond the training horizon. Moreover, by coupling the two flows through shared summary statistics, AFSF induces an implicit regularization across latent state trajectories and improves trajectory-level smoothing. In addition, we develop a flow-based particle filtering variant that provides an alternative filtering procedure and enables ESS-based diagnostics when explicit model factors are available. Numerical experiments demonstrate that AFSF provides accurate approximations of both filtering distributions and smoothing paths.

</details>


### [99] [The Theory and Practice of Highly Scalable Gaussian Process Regression with Nearest Neighbours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07267)
*Robert Allison,Tomasz Maciazek,Anthony Stephenson*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文为最近邻高斯过程（NNGP/GPnn）回归建立了理论框架，证明了其在大样本下的统计性质，包括点预测极限、一致性、收敛速率和超参数鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程回归在训练规模上具有立方复杂度，限制了其在大规模数据集上的应用。最近邻高斯过程（NNGP/GPnn）通过仅使用每个测试点的最近邻进行预测，在实践中表现出色，但其大样本理论仍不完整。需要建立严格的理论基础来支持NNGP/GPnn作为可扩展且原理性的高斯过程替代方法。

Method: 开发了一个理论框架来分析NNGP和GPnn回归。在温和的正则性假设下，推导了三个关键预测标准（均方误差MSE、校准系数CAL和负对数似然NLL）的几乎必然点极限。研究了L2风险，证明了普遍一致性，并分析了风险收敛速率。还证明了MSE在紧致超参数集上的一致收敛性及其对超参数导数的渐近性质。

Result: 证明了NNGP/GPnn的MSE、CAL和NLL具有几乎必然点极限；L2风险达到Stone的最小最大速率n^{-2α/(2p+d)}，其中α和p捕捉回归问题的正则性；MSE在紧致超参数集上一致收敛；MSE对长度尺度、核尺度和噪声方差的导数渐近消失，具有显式速率。这些结果解释了GPnn对超参数调优的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究为NNGP/GPnn作为高度可扩展且原理性的全高斯过程模型替代方法提供了严格的统计基础。理论结果解释了其经验性能，特别是对超参数调优的鲁棒性，使其成为大规模高斯过程回归的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Gaussian process ($GP$) regression is a widely used non-parametric modeling tool, but its cubic complexity in the training size limits its use on massive data sets. A practical remedy is to predict using only the nearest neighbours of each test point, as in Nearest Neighbour Gaussian Process ($NNGP$) regression for geospatial problems and the related scalable $GPnn$ method for more general machine-learning applications. Despite their strong empirical performance, the large-$n$ theory of $NNGP/GPnn$ remains incomplete. We develop a theoretical framework for $NNGP$ and $GPnn$ regression. Under mild regularity assumptions, we derive almost sure pointwise limits for three key predictive criteria: mean squared error ($MSE$), calibration coefficient ($CAL$), and negative log-likelihood ($NLL$). We then study the $L_2$-risk, prove universal consistency, and show that the risk attains Stone's minimax rate $n^{-2α/(2p+d)}$, where $α$ and $p$ capture regularity of the regression problem. We also prove uniform convergence of $MSE$ over compact hyper-parameter sets and show that its derivatives with respect to lengthscale, kernel scale, and noise variance vanish asymptotically, with explicit rates. This explains the observed robustness of $GPnn$ to hyper-parameter tuning. These results provide a rigorous statistical foundation for $NNGP/GPnn$ as a highly scalable and principled alternative to full $GP$ models.

</details>


### [100] [Gaussian Approximation for Asynchronous Q-learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07323)
*Artemy Rubtsov,Sergey Samsonov,Vladimir Ulyanov,Alexey Naumov*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文推导了异步Q学习算法中Polyak-Ruppert平均迭代在高维中心极限定理下的收敛速率，使用多项式步长k^{-ω}，在均匀几何遍历马尔可夫链假设下，获得了超矩形类上高达n^{-1/6}log^4(nSA)的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究异步Q学习算法中Polyak-Ruppert平均迭代的高维中心极限定理收敛速率，为强化学习算法的统计推断提供理论保证。

Method: 使用多项式步长k^{-ω}（ω∈(1/2,1]）的异步Q学习算法，假设状态-动作-下一状态三元组序列形成均匀几何遍历马尔可夫链，证明高维中心极限定理并推导收敛速率。

Result: 在超矩形类上获得高达n^{-1/6}log^4(nSA)的收敛速率，其中n为样本数，S和A分别为状态和动作数量。同时证明了高维中心极限定理并给出了算法最后迭代的高阶矩边界。

Conclusion: 本文为异步Q学习算法的Polyak-Ruppert平均迭代建立了高维中心极限定理收敛速率，为强化学习算法的统计推断提供了理论基础，证明的鞅差和的高维中心极限定理具有独立的理论价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we derive rates of convergence in the high-dimensional central limit theorem for Polyak-Ruppert averaged iterates generated by the asynchronous Q-learning algorithm with a polynomial stepsize $k^{-ω},\, ω\in (1/2, 1]$. Assuming that the sequence of state-action-next-state triples $(s_k, a_k, s_{k+1})_{k \geq 0}$ forms a uniformly geometrically ergodic Markov chain, we establish a rate of order up to $n^{-1/6} \log^{4} (nS A)$ over the class of hyper-rectangles, where $n$ is the number of samples used by the algorithm and $S$ and $A$ denote the numbers of states and actions, respectively. To obtain this result, we prove a high-dimensional central limit theorem for sums of martingale differences, which may be of independent interest. Finally, we present bounds for high-order moments for the algorithm's last iterate.

</details>


### [101] [Sobolev Norm Learning Rates for Conditional Mean Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2105.07446)
*Prem Talwai,Ali Shameli,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文为条件均值嵌入开发了新的学习率，通过应用再生核希尔伯特空间插值理论，在目标算子非希尔伯特-施密特或无界的情况下，推导出样本估计器的显式自适应收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件均值嵌入方法通常假设目标算子在输入/输出RKHS中是希尔伯特-施密特或有界的，这限制了其在更复杂的机器学习/强化学习场景中的应用，特别是涉及无限维RKHS和连续状态空间的情况。

Method: 应用再生核希尔伯特空间插值理论，在目标算子非希尔伯特-施密特或无界的情况下，推导条件均值嵌入样本估计器的收敛率。方法能够处理模型误设情况，并在某些参数范围内实现输出RKHS中的一致收敛。

Result: 获得了显式的自适应收敛率，这些收敛率适用于误设情况。在某些参数范围内，能够在输出RKHS中实现一致收敛率，突破了传统方法的限制。

Conclusion: 该分析扩展了条件均值嵌入的应用范围，使其能够应用于更复杂的ML/RL场景，包括无限维RKHS和连续状态空间，为条件均值嵌入在更广泛环境中的应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop novel learning rates for conditional mean embeddings by applying the theory of interpolation for reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHS). We derive explicit, adaptive convergence rates for the sample estimator under the misspecifed setting, where the target operator is not Hilbert-Schmidt or bounded with respect to the input/output RKHSs. We demonstrate that in certain parameter regimes, we can achieve uniform convergence rates in the output RKHS. We hope our analyses will allow the much broader application of conditional mean embeddings to more complex ML/RL settings involving infinite dimensional RKHSs and continuous state spaces.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Non-asymptotic two-sample kernel testing with the spectrally truncated normalized MMD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07153)
*Perrine Lacroix,Bertrand Michel,Franck Picard,Vincent Rivoirard*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究核两样本检验问题，提出了一种基于谱截断归一化最大均值差异（st-nMMD）的检验方法，推导了其在零假设下的非渐近指数上界，并开发了相应的分位数估计和超参数调优算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核方法通过将概率分布嵌入到再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中，为非参数统计检验提供了灵活而强大的框架。归一化的最大均值差异（MMD）通过按组内协方差算子缩放差异来考虑数据变异性，已被证明在理论和实证中都能提高检验功效。然而，这种归一化需要正则化，因此需要研究其非渐近性质。

Method: 研究谱截断归一化MMD（st-nMMD）的非渐近性质，推导其在零假设下的指数上界。基于这一结果，提出了相应的非渐近分位数的尖锐显式上界，并开发了数据自适应估计器。进一步提出了一个算法来调优分位数估计中涉及的超参数（包括截断水平），而无需数据分割。

Result: 推导出了st-nMMD在零假设下的指数上界，获得了相应的非渐近分位数的尖锐显式上界。提出的数据自适应估计器和超参数调优算法在数值实验中表现出良好性能，在零假设和备择假设下都得到了验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为核两样本检验提供了一种基于谱截断归一化MMD的有效方法，通过理论分析和算法设计解决了归一化MMD的正则化问题，并在非渐近框架下获得了可证明的统计保证，为实际应用提供了实用的检验工具。

Abstract: Kernel methods provide a flexible and powerful framework for nonparametric statistical testing by embedding probability distributions into a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS). In this work, we study the kernel two-sample testing problem and focus on a normalized version of the Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) as a test statistic, which scales the discrepancy by the within-group covariance operator to account for data variability. This normalization has been shown to improve test power in both theoretical and empirical settings. Because this normalization requires regularization, we study the non-asymptotic properties of the spectrally truncated normalized MMD (st-nMMD) and derive an exponential upper bound under the null hypothesis. Thanks to this result we propose a sharp and explicit upper bound for the corresponding non-asymptotic quantile, along with a data-adaptive estimator. We further propose an algorithm to tune the hyperparameters involved in the quantile estimation, including the truncation level, without requiring data splitting. We demonstrate the performance of the st-nMMD through numerical experiments under both the null and alternative hypotheses.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Bridging Theory and Practice in Crafting Robust Spiking Reservoirs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06395)
*Ruggero Freddi,Nicolas Seseri,Diana Nigrisoli,Alessio Basti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究引入鲁棒性区间概念，用于量化脉冲储备池计算在边缘混沌状态下的稳定操作范围，发现连接密度和放电阈值对鲁棒性区间的单调影响规律，验证了理论临界点在实际任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲储备池计算虽然能效高，但由于实验不确定性，难以可靠地将储备池调谐到边缘混沌状态。需要建立抽象临界性概念与实际稳定性之间的桥梁，为参数调优提供实用指导。

Method: 引入鲁棒性区间作为操作度量，系统评估LIF架构在静态(MNIST)和时序(合成球轨迹)任务上的表现。分析连接密度β和放电阈值θ对鲁棒性区间的影响，识别保持理论临界点w_crit的(β, θ)参数对。在Erdős-Rényi图上进行控制实验验证拓扑无关性。

Result: 鲁棒性区间宽度随突触前连接密度β增加而减小(即与稀疏性正相关)，随放电阈值θ增加而增大。识别出保持w_crit的(β, θ)参数对，形成超参数空间中的等性能流形。w_crit始终位于经验高性能区域内，验证其作为参数搜索起点的有效性。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性区间为脉冲储备池计算的稳定性提供了实用度量，揭示了连接密度和放电阈值的系统性影响规律。理论临界点w_crit在实际任务中具有鲁棒性，可作为参数调优的可靠起点，简化边缘混沌状态下的储备池配置。

Abstract: Spiking reservoir computing provides an energy-efficient approach to temporal processing, but reliably tuning reservoirs to operate at the edge-of-chaos is challenging due to experimental uncertainty. This work bridges abstract notions of criticality and practical stability by introducing and exploiting the robustness interval, an operational measure of the hyperparameter range over which a reservoir maintains performance above task-dependent thresholds. Through systematic evaluations of Leaky Integrate-and-Fire (LIF) architectures on both static (MNIST) and temporal (synthetic Ball Trajectories) tasks, we identify consistent monotonic trends in the robustness interval across a broad spectrum of network configurations: the robustness-interval width decreases with presynaptic connection density $β$ (i.e., directly with sparsity) and directly with the firing threshold $θ$. We further identify specific $(β, θ)$ pairs that preserve the analytical mean-field critical point $w_{\text{crit}}$, revealing iso-performance manifolds in the hyperparameter space. Control experiments on Erdős-Rényi graphs show the phenomena persist beyond small-world topologies. Finally, our results show that $w_{\text{crit}}$ consistently falls within empirical high-performance regions, validating $w_{\text{crit}}$ as a robust starting coordinate for parameter search and fine-tuning. To ensure reproducibility, the full Python code is publicly available.

</details>


### [104] [Weighted Bayesian Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06464)
*Xiayin Lou,Peng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WBCP将贝叶斯正交化共形预测扩展到加权设置，通过加权狄利克雷分布处理分布偏移，提供数据条件保证和丰富的不确定性信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BQ-CP需要i.i.d.假设，而加权共形预测虽然能处理分布偏移但只提供阈值点估计。需要一种能结合两者优势的方法，在非i.i.d.设置下提供贝叶斯不确定性量化。

Method: 提出加权贝叶斯共形预测(WBCP)，用加权狄利克雷分布Dir(neff·w̃₁,...,neff·w̃ₙ)替代均匀狄利克雷分布，其中neff是Kish有效样本量。将BQ-CP推广到任意重要性加权设置。

Result: 证明了四个理论结果：neff是匹配频率派和贝叶斯方差的唯一浓度参数；后验标准差以O(1/√neff)衰减；BQ-CP的随机支配保证扩展到每个权重配置的数据条件保证；HPD阈值在条件覆盖上提供O(1/√neff)改进。

Conclusion: WBCP在保持覆盖保证的同时提供更丰富的不确定性信息，特别适用于空间预测等非i.i.d.场景，通过地理BQ-CP实例化展示了实用价值。

Abstract: Conformal prediction provides distribution-free prediction intervals with finite-sample coverage guarantees, and recent work by Snell \& Griffiths reframes it as Bayesian Quadrature (BQ-CP), yielding powerful data-conditional guarantees via Dirichlet posteriors over thresholds. However, BQ-CP fundamentally requires the i.i.d. assumption -- a limitation the authors themselves identify. Meanwhile, weighted conformal prediction handles distribution shift via importance weights but remains frequentist, producing only point-estimate thresholds. We propose \textbf{Weighted Bayesian Conformal Prediction (WBCP)}, which generalizes BQ-CP to arbitrary importance-weighted settings by replacing the uniform Dirichlet $\Dir(1,\ldots,1)$ with a weighted Dirichlet $\Dir(\neff \cdot \tilde{w}_1, \ldots, \neff \cdot \tilde{w}_n)$, where $\neff$ is Kish's effective sample size. We prove four theoretical results: (1)~$\neff$ is the unique concentration parameter matching frequentist and Bayesian variances; (2)~posterior standard deviation decays as $O(1/\sqrt{\neff})$; (3)~BQ-CP's stochastic dominance guarantee extends to per-weight-profile data-conditional guarantees; (4)~the HPD threshold provides $O(1/\sqrt{\neff})$ improvement in conditional coverage. We instantiate WBCP for spatial prediction as \emph{Geographical BQ-CP}, where kernel-based spatial weights yield per-location posteriors with interpretable diagnostics. Experiments on synthetic and real-world spatial datasets demonstrate that WBCP maintains coverage guarantees while providing substantially richer uncertainty information.

</details>


### [105] [Conformal Margin Risk Minimization: An Envelope Framework for Robust Learning under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06468)
*Yuanjie Shi,Peihong Li,Zijian Zhang,Janardhan Rao Doppa,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CMRM是一个无需先验知识的插件式框架，通过添加分位数校准的正则化项来改进标签噪声下的分类损失，利用置信度边缘和保形分位数阈值来聚焦高边缘样本并抑制可能误标的样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理标签噪声的方法通常需要先验知识（如噪声转移矩阵、干净子集或预训练特征提取器），而这些资源在鲁棒性最需要时往往不可用，因此需要一种无需特权知识的方法。

Method: 提出保形边缘风险最小化（CMRM）框架，测量观测标签与竞争标签之间的置信度边缘，并使用每批次估计的保形分位数进行阈值处理，通过添加单个分位数校准的正则化项来聚焦高边缘样本并抑制可能误标的样本。

Result: 在5个基础方法和6个基准测试（包含合成和真实世界噪声）中，CMRM持续提升准确率（最高+3.39%），减少保形预测集大小（最高-20.44%），且在0%噪声下不会损害性能，表明CMRM捕获了现有机制未利用的方法无关的不确定性信号。

Conclusion: CMRM是一个无需特权知识或训练流程修改的插件式框架，能够有效改善标签噪声下的分类性能，通过保形边缘阈值处理提供了一种通用的不确定性信号利用机制。

Abstract: Most methods for learning with noisy labels require privileged knowledge such as noise transition matrices, clean subsets or pretrained feature extractors, resources typically unavailable when robustness is most needed. We propose Conformal Margin Risk Minimization (CMRM), a plug-and-play envelope framework that improves any classification loss under label noise by adding a single quantile-calibrated regularization term, with no privileged knowledge or training pipeline modification. CMRM measures the confidence margin between the observed label and competing labels, and thresholds it with a conformal quantile estimated per batch to focus training on high-margin samples while suppressing likely mislabeled ones. We derive a learning bound for CMRM under arbitrary label noise requiring only mild regularity of the margin distribution. Across five base methods and six benchmarks with synthetic and real-world noise, CMRM consistently improves accuracy (up to +3.39%), reduces conformal prediction set size (up to -20.44%) and does not hurt under 0% noise, showing that CMRM captures a method-agnostic uncertainty signal that existing mechanisms did not exploit.

</details>


### [106] [Optimal Rates for Pure {\varepsilon}-Differentially Private Stochastic Convex Optimization with Heavy Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06492)
*Andrew Lowy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有重尾梯度的随机凸优化在纯ε-差分隐私下的问题，通过假设有界k阶矩而非有界Lipschitz参数，获得了更尖锐的过风险界，并首次刻画了纯ε-DP重尾SCO的极小极大最优过风险率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注近似(ε,δ)-DP下的重尾随机凸优化，而纯ε-DP情况的最优过风险率尚未解决。传统方法假设有界Lipschitz参数，这限制了处理无界重尾随机梯度分布的能力。本文旨在通过仅假设有界k阶矩来获得更尖锐的过风险界，并填补纯ε-DP重尾SCO的理论空白。

Method: 提出了一种基于私有优化经验损失的Lipschitz扩展的新框架。该方法不依赖有界Lipschitz参数，而是假设有界k阶矩，从而能够处理无界重尾随机梯度分布。算法以高概率在多项式时间内实现最优率，对于某些结构问题类（如铰链/ReLU型和绝对值损失），即使最坏情况Lipschitz参数无限，也能以概率1在多项式时间内实现相同保证。

Result: 首次刻画了纯ε-DP重尾随机凸优化的极小极大最优过风险率（达到对数因子）。算法以高概率在多项式时间内实现该最优率。当最坏情况Lipschitz参数多项式有界时，算法以概率1在多项式时间内运行。对于重要结构问题类，即使最坏情况Lipschitz参数无限，也能以概率1在多项式时间内实现相同的过风险保证。

Conclusion: 本文解决了纯ε-DP重尾随机凸优化的理论空白，通过仅假设有界k阶矩而非有界Lipschitz参数，获得了更尖锐的过风险界。提出的新框架能够处理无界重尾随机梯度分布，并为重要结构问题类提供了多项式时间算法保证。同时提供了新的高概率下界作为补充。

Abstract: We study stochastic convex optimization (SCO) with heavy-tailed gradients under pure epsilon-differential privacy (DP). Instead of assuming a bound on the worst-case Lipschitz parameter of the loss, we assume only a bounded k-th moment. This assumption allows for unbounded, heavy-tailed stochastic gradient distributions, and can yield sharper excess risk bounds. The minimax optimal rate for approximate (epsilon, delta)-DP SCO is known in this setting, but the pure epsilon-DP case has remained open. We characterize the minimax optimal excess-risk rate for pure epsilon-DP heavy-tailed SCO up to logarithmic factors. Our algorithm achieves this rate in polynomial time with high probability. Moreover, it runs in polynomial time with probability 1 when the worst-case Lipschitz parameter is polynomially bounded. For important structured problem classes - including hinge/ReLU-type and absolute-value losses on Euclidean balls, ellipsoids, and polytopes - we achieve the same excess-risk guarantee in polynomial time with probability 1 even when the worst-case Lipschitz parameter is infinite. Our approach is based on a novel framework for privately optimizing Lipschitz extensions of the empirical loss. We complement our excess risk upper bound with a novel high probability lower bound.

</details>


### [107] [Bi-Lipschitz Autoencoder With Injectivity Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06701)
*Qipeng Zhan,Zhuoping Zhou,Zexuan Wang,Qi Long,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Bi-Lipschitz自编码器(BLAE)，通过注入性正则化和双Lipschitz松弛解决传统正则化自编码器的非单射映射问题，提升流形结构保持能力和对数据分布漂移的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统正则化自编码器在降维时假设高维数据位于低维流形上，但现有方法常存在非单射映射问题，导致收敛困难和潜在表示失真。此外，过于刚性的约束限制了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Bi-Lipschitz自编码器(BLAE)，包含两个关键创新：(1)基于分离准则的注入性正则化方案，消除病态局部最小值；(2)双Lipschitz松弛，保持几何结构并展现对数据分布漂移的鲁棒性。同时形式化了可接受正则化的概念并提供了满足条件的充分条件。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验结果表明，BLAE在保持流形结构方面持续优于现有方法，同时对采样稀疏性和分布漂移具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: BLAE通过解决编码器非单射性这一核心瓶颈，提供了更有效的流形保持降维方法，具有更好的收敛性和对数据分布变化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autoencoders are widely used for dimensionality reduction, based on the assumption that high-dimensional data lies on low-dimensional manifolds. Regularized autoencoders aim to preserve manifold geometry during dimensionality reduction, but existing approaches often suffer from non-injective mappings and overly rigid constraints that limit their effectiveness and robustness. In this work, we identify encoder non-injectivity as a core bottleneck that leads to poor convergence and distorted latent representations. To ensure robustness across data distributions, we formalize the concept of admissible regularization and provide sufficient conditions for its satisfaction. In this work, we propose the Bi-Lipschitz Autoencoder (BLAE), which introduces two key innovations: (1) an injective regularization scheme based on a separation criterion to eliminate pathological local minima, and (2) a bi-Lipschitz relaxation that preserves geometry and exhibits robustness to data distribution drift. Empirical results on diverse datasets show that BLAE consistently outperforms existing methods in preserving manifold structure while remaining resilient to sampling sparsity and distribution shifts. Code is available at https://github.com/qipengz/BLAE.

</details>


### [108] [Are Stochastic Multi-objective Bandits Harder than Single-objective Bandits?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07096)
*Changkun Guan,Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明在随机多目标赌博机问题中，帕累托遗憾由最大次优差距g†主导，而非维度数，并提出了最优算法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标赌博机中帕累托遗憾是否比单目标问题更难优化存在争议，现有研究表明随机设置中帕累托遗憾随维度增加，这引发了核心问题：多目标赌博机是否真的比单目标更难？

Method: 提出新算法，采用嵌套两层不确定性量化（通过上下置信界估计器），结合top-two racing策略进行臂选择和不确定性贪婪规则进行维度选择，平衡两层探索与利用

Result: 证明随机设置中帕累托遗憾由最大次优差距g†主导，达到O(K log T/g†)的帕累托遗憾，这是最优的；数值实验验证了算法性能优于基准方法

Conclusion: 多目标赌博机在随机设置中并不比单目标更难，帕累托遗憾由最大次优差距而非维度数决定，提出的算法达到最优性能

Abstract: Multi-objective bandits have attracted increasing attention because of their broad applicability and mathematical elegance, where the reward of each arm is a multi-dimensional vector rather than a scalar. This naturally introduces Pareto order relations and Pareto regret. A long-standing question in this area is whether performance is fundamentally harder to optimize because of this added complexity. A recent surprising result shows that, in the adversarial setting, Pareto regret is no larger than classical regret; however, in the stochastic setting, where the regret notion is different, the picture remains unclear. In fact, existing work suggests that Pareto regret in the stochastic case increases with the dimensionality. This controversial yet subtle phenomenon motivates our central question: \emph{are multi-objective bandits actually harder than single-objective ones?} We answer this question in full by showing that, in the stochastic setting, Pareto regret is in fact governed by the maximum sub-optimality gap \(g^\dagger\), and hence by the minimum marginal regret of order \(Ω(\frac{K\log T}{g^\dagger})\). We further develop a new algorithm that achieves Pareto regret of order \(O(\frac{K\log T}{g^\dagger})\), and is therefore optimal. The algorithm leverages a nested two-layer uncertainty quantification over both arms and objectives through upper and lower confidence bound estimators. It combines a top-two racing strategy for arm selection with an uncertainty-greedy rule for dimension selection. Together, these components balance exploration and exploitation across the two layers. We also conduct comprehensive numerical experiments to validate the proposed algorithm, showing the desired regret guarantee and significant gains over benchmark methods.

</details>


### [109] [FLeX: Fourier-based Low-rank EXpansion for multilingual transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06253)
*Gaurav Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了使用参数高效微调方法（LoRA）和优化器增强技术（Sophia vs Adam）以及傅里叶正则化来改进从Python到Java等语言的跨语言代码生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在企业环境中，多种编程语言共存使得跨语言代码生成至关重要，但为每种语言单独微调大型语言模型计算成本过高。因此需要探索参数高效的跨语言迁移方法。

Method: 使用Code Llama 7B模型，采用低秩适应（LoRA）微调仅优化少量参数，比较Adam和Sophia优化器，并探索基于傅里叶的正则化技术。在MBPP数据集上进行实验。

Result: 1) LoRA微调在小型高质量数据集上的pass@1性能（40.1%）超过了广泛微调的Code Llama-Python-7B模型（38.4%）；2) Sophia比Adam收敛更快，但最终性能差异不大；3) 傅里叶正则化显著提升跨语言迁移，在Java任务上达到42.1% pass@1，相比基线34.2%。

Conclusion: 结合LoRA、优化训练方法和频域正则化可以高效地将单语言LLM适配到多种编程语言，实现计算高效的跨语言代码生成。

Abstract: Cross-lingual code generation is critical in enterprise environments where multiple programming languages coexist. However, fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) individually for each language is computationally prohibitive. This paper investigates whether parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods and optimizer enhancements can improve cross-lingual transfer from Python to languages like Java. We fine-tune the Code Llama 7B model using low-rank adaptation (LoRA) to optimize a small subset of parameters and compare Adam and Sophia optimizers, while exploring a novel Fourier-based regularization technique. Our contributions include: (1)demonstrating that LoRA fine-tuning on a small, high-quality dataset (MBPP) can exceed the pass@1 performance of the more broadly fine-tuned Code Llama-Python-7B model (40.1% vs. 38.4%); (2) showing that while Sophia achieves faster convergence than Adam, final pass@1 scores show marginal differences; and (3) presenting evidence that Fourier-based regularization during fine-tuning significantly improves cross-lingual transfer, achieving 42.1% pass@1 on Java tasks compared to the 34.2% baseline. These findings suggest that combining LoRA, optimized training methods, and frequency-domain regularization can efficiently adapt single-language LLMs to perform well across multiple programming languages.

</details>


### [110] [MO-RiskVAE: A Multi-Omics Variational Autoencoder for Survival Risk Modeling in Multiple MyelomaMO-RiskVAE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06267)
*Zixuan Chen,Heng Zhang,YuPeng Qin,WenPeng Xing,Qiang Wang,Da Wang,Changting Lin,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.LG
